# Graph Report - agent-verse-backend  (2026-09-09)

## Corpus Check
- 2850 files · ~2,282,930 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 47158 nodes · 111829 edges · 1624 communities (1249 shown, 107 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 94% EXTRACTED · 6% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6604 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `085b2ee2`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
